- **di-agent-bug-report**: Generates a Markdown bug report for an IBM watsonx.data integration session. User can invoke directly. Skip for non-watsonx.data integration sessions. - **di-agent-datastage-investigation**: Deep investigation of a failed run of a DataStage job already in operation — a scheduled or production job, or any job whose failure the user asks you to investigate. Works end-to-end from MCP tools: logs, job config, flow structure, connection health. Produces a root-cause diagnosis, not a fix. NOT for a run that failed while a flow was being built or edited — those are handled by the di-agent-flow-lifecycle skill, which runs its own investigation and loads this one only when a production-depth analysis is needed. ([IBM/ibm-watsonx-data-integration-skills](https://github.com/IBM/ibm-watsonx-data-integration-skills/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/di-agent-datastage-investigation) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **di-agent-flow-datastage**: Reference for the verbose watsonx.data integration SDK for DataStage (batch) flows, with exhaustive stage and property access — the full engine stage catalog. Load this only after the di-agent-flow-lifecycle router has selected the datastage-sdk authoring backend — this is a syntax and stage reference, not a decision about whether to use it. If you have not routed yet, load di-agent-flow-lifecycle instead. ([IBM/ibm-watsonx-data-integration-skills](https://github.com/IBM/ibm-watsonx-data-integration-skills/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/di-agent-flow-datastage) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **di-agent-flow-lifecycle**: Entry point for ALL flow work in watsonx.data integration — creating, editing, running, diagnosing a failed run, or backing up/restoring a flow. Load this FIRST whenever a request touches a data flow. It gathers intent — including which engine the flow runs on — routes to exactly one lifecycle state, and applies a fixed precedence order so routing is consistent run to run. ([IBM/ibm-watsonx-data-integration-skills](https://github.com/IBM/ibm-watsonx-data-integration-skills/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/di-agent-flow-lifecycle) · ★1 · 2 copies)
